- **kiln-harness-tuner-knowledge**: The Kiln Harness Tuner knowledge system - what to try when you tune an agent's harness. Smells (issues in the design of the agent), playbooks (how to solve one problem or reach one goal), techniques (one optimisation each) and levers (what the tuner can change, and how). Each loop role has its own entry file in references/ - planner.md, builder.md, smells.md, analyzer.md, report.md - and technique-index.md is only for a technique that the user's guidance names. Your prompt template names your entry file. No other files exist at the top of references/. ([Kiln-AI/harness-tuner](https://github.com/Kiln-AI/harness-tuner/tree/HEAD/src/kiln_harness_tuner/agent_files/skills/kiln-harness-tuner-knowledge) · ★18)

- **kiln-log**: The only writer of a task journal at .kiln/memory/tasks/&lt;n&gt;-&lt;slug&gt;.md. Creates it at kiln-start, appends the row every other skill hands over, records the verify report, decisions, skips and retro notes, and commits it with "📝 docs(kiln): log &lt;skill&gt; for #&lt;n&gt;". Use at the end of every kiln skill, and for the final pass before gate 2. ([pavelsimo/kiln](https://github.com/pavelsimo/kiln/tree/HEAD/.kiln/skills/kiln-log) · 2 copies)
- **kiln-merge**: Holds gate 2. Use at the end of the Ship stage once kiln-watch has the PR green; INCONCLUSIVE verdicts need explicit human acceptance. ([pavelsimo/kiln](https://github.com/pavelsimo/kiln/tree/HEAD/.kiln/skills/kiln-merge) · 2 copies)

Do NOT trigger when the user points to a specific product documentation site — use the scraper-workflow skill for that. Do NOT trigger when the user wants to query already-indexed content — use the king-context skill (`kctx search`) for that. ([deandevz/king-context](https://github.com/deandevz/king-context/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/king-research) · ★59)
- **king-research**: Build topic-driven King Context research corpora with `.king-context/bin/king-research`. Use when the user asks to research a topic, build a corpus, find sources, create specialist knowledge bases, or gather open-web background for a workflow. - **king-type-driven-design**: Parse, don't validate: raw data becomes rich types at the boundary, illegal states unrepresentable, total functions. Use when designing domain types or input handling, or reviewing validation that returns booleans, scattered checks, or flag-based state. ([aleemont1/TheCouncil](https://github.com/aleemont1/TheCouncil/tree/HEAD/skills/types/king-type-driven-design) · ★1)
- **kingbase-awr-report**: 给定 KingbaseES（金仓）数据库连接串（host/port/dbname/user/password 或 postgresql:// URI），以资深 KingbaseES DBA 专家视角连接数据库，采集两个时间点的系统计数器视图并做差，生成类似 Oracle AWR 报告的 KingbaseES 性能诊断报告（Load Profile、Top SQL、等待事件、实例效率、Checkpoint/BGWriter、锁等待、复制延迟、表膨胀、配置快照、Findings & Recommendations）。KingbaseES 默认采用 PG 兼容模式，因此 SQL/视图与 PostgreSQL 高度一致，但 Top SQL 取自 `sys_stat_statements` 而非 `pg_stat_statements`，且 kingbase 自带 `sys_kwr` 自动快照仓库可作交叉验证。触发条件：用户提到「生成AWR报告」「KingbaseES性能报告」「金仓AWR」「金仓性能报告」「金仓数据库健康检查」「金仓数据库体检」「给你连接串帮我看看这个金仓库」「Top SQL分析」「等待事件分析」「sys_stat_statements 分析」「金仓数据库负载画像」「类似Oracle AWR」「金仓性能诊断报告」「帮我诊断一下这个KingbaseES实例」「sys_kwr… ([digoal/skills](https://github.com/digoal/skills/tree/HEAD/kingbase/kingbase-awr-report) · ★42)
